i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format.\n//   Rows are independent, so the loop is distributed over OpenMP threads.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Kernel: spmvKernel\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   CSR SpMV on the GPU. A group of THREADS_PER_ROW consecutive threads\n//   cooperates on one row; the group size is chosen at launch time from the\n//   average number of non-zeros per row so that memory accesses stay\n//   coalesced without wasting lanes on short rows.\n//\n//   rowPtr is rebased to the local non-zero slice, i.e. rowPtr[0] == 0.\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int THREADS_PER_ROW>\n__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowPtr, const double* __restrict__ vec,\n                           const index_t numLocalRows, double* __restrict__ out) {\n    const unsigned int laneInWarp = threadIdx.x & 31u;\n    const int lane = static_cast<int>(laneInWarp) % THREADS_PER_ROW;\n\n    // All threads of a group work on the same row, so the group mask is uniform\n    const unsigned int groupMask =\n        (THREADS_PER_ROW == 32)\n            ? 0xffffffffu\n            : (((1u << THREADS_PER_ROW) - 1u) << (laneInWarp - static_cast<unsigned>(lane)));\n\n    const index_t groupsPerBlock = blockDim.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;\n    const index_t group = blockIdx.x * groupsPerBlock + threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;\n    const index_t stride = groupsPerBlock * gridDim.x;\n\n    for (index_t row = group; row < numLocalRows; row += stride) {\n        const index_t rowBegin = rowPtr[row];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowPtr[row + 1];\n\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowBegin + lane; j < rowEnd; j += THREADS_PER_ROW) {\n            t += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);\n        }\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            t += __shfl_down_sync(groupMask, t, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);\n        }\n\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            out[row] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: launchSpmv\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Dispatches the templated SpMV kernel with a group size matching the\n//   average row length.\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid launchSpmv(const double* d_val, const index_t* d_cols, const index_t* d_rowPtr,\n                const double* d_vec, const index_t numLocalRows, double* d_out,\n                const double avgNnzPerRow, cudaStream_t stream) {\n    constexpr int BLOCK = 256;\n    constexpr index_t MAX_BLOCKS = 65535 * 8;\n\n    if (numLocalRows == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const auto launch = [&](auto threadsPerRow) {\n        constexpr int T = decltype(threadsPerRow)::value;\n        const index_t groupsPerBlock = BLOCK / T;\n        const index_t blocks = std::min<index_t>(\n            MAX_BLOCKS, (numLocalRows + groupsPerBlock - 1) / groupsPerBlock);\n        spmvKernel<T><<<blocks, BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowPtr, d_vec, numLocalRows,\n                                                    d_out);\n    };\n\n    if (avgNnzPerRow <= 4.0) {\n        launch(std::integral_constant<int, 2>{});\n    } else if (avgNnzPerRow <= 8.0) {\n        launch(std::integral_constant<int, 4>{});\n    } else if (avgNnzPerRow <= 16.0) {\n        launch(std::integral_constant<int, 8>{});\n    } else if (avgNnzPerRow <= 32.0) {\n        launch(std::integral_constant<int, 16>{});\n    } else {\n        launch(std::integral_constant<int, 32>{});\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution.\n//   The scan runs in parallel; the smallest mismatching index is reported so\n//   that the diagnostic output matches the sequential scan.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    index_t firstBad = size;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : firstBad)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        bool bad = false;\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            bad = std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR;\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            bad = std::abs((res - ref) / ref) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR;\n        }\n        if (bad && i < firstBad) {\n            firstBad = i;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (firstBad == size) {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    const double ref = reference[firstBad];\n    const double res = result[firstBad];\n    if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", firstBad, ref, res);\n    } else {\n        const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n        printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n               firstBad, ref, res, relError);\n    }\n    return false;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: partitionByNnz\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Splits the row range [rowBegin, rowEnd) into `parts` contiguous blocks\n//   holding a comparable number of non-zeros each, which balances the SpMV\n//   work between MPI ranks and between GPUs.\n//\n// Returns:\n//   Vector of parts+1 row boundaries.\n// ****************************************************************************\nstd::vector<index_t> partitionByNnz(const index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t rowBegin,\n                                    const index_t rowEnd, const int parts) {\n    std::vector<index_t> bounds(parts + 1);\n    bounds[0] = rowBegin;\n    bounds[parts] = rowEnd;\n\n    const index_t nnzBegin = rowDelimiters[rowBegin];\n    const index_t nnzTotal = rowDelimiters[rowEnd] - nnzBegin;\n\n    for (int p = 1; p < parts; ++p) {\n        const index_t target =\n            nnzBegin + static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nnzTotal) * p) / parts);\n        const index_t* it =\n            std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters + bounds[p - 1], rowDelimiters + rowEnd, target);\n        index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(it - rowDelimiters);\n\n        // Keep boundaries monotonic and leave one row per remaining part where possible\n        const index_t remaining = static_cast<index_t>(parts - p);\n        const index_t maxRow = (rowEnd - rowBegin >= remaining) ? rowEnd - remaining : rowBegin;\n        bounds[p] = std::max(bounds[p - 1], std::min(row, maxRow));\n    }\n    return bounds;\n}\n\n// Sends/receives element counts that may exceed the int range of MPI counts\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid sendLarge(const T* buf, const size_t count, const int dest, const int tag, MPI_Datatype type) {\n    for (size_t off = 0; off < count; off += MPI_CHUNK) {\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(MPI_CHUNK, count - off));\n        MPI_Send(buf + off, n, type, dest, tag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid recvLarge(T* buf, const size_t count, const int src, const int tag, MPI_Datatype type) {\n    for d_out = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiThreadLevel = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiThreadLevel);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n rank)\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if A group of THREADS_PER_ROW consecutive threads\n//   cooperates on one row; the group size is chosen at launch time from the\n//   average number of non-zeros per row so that memory accesses stay\n//   coalesced without wasting lanes on short rows.\n//\n//   rowPtr is rebased to the local non-zero slice, i.e. rowPtr[0] == 0.\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int THREADS_PER_ROW>\n__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ const index_t groupsPerBlock = blockDim.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;\n    const index_t group = blockIdx.x * groupsPerBlock + threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;\n    const index_t stride = groupsPerBlock * THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            t += __shfl_down_sync(groupMask, t, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);\n        }\n\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            out[row] = t;\n ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    index_t firstBad = {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Ceg6Kky4D3XVqaqN86Ajh","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The build directory (`spmv/build`) is left in place since removing it needs your approval — it's only artifacts.\n\n## What changed\n\n**`spmv/spmv.cpp`** — unconditional MPI + OpenMP + CUDA:\n\n- **MPI**: rank 0 generates the matrix (the `rand()` sequence must stay serial to reproduce the original problem bit-for-bit), broadcasts `rowDelimiters` and the dense vector, then ships each rank its CSR slice via non-blocking chunked sends (chunked so counts stay within MPI's `int` range for very large matrices). Rows are partitioned into contiguous blocks **balanced by non-zero count**, not row count, via `partitionByNnz`. Results come back with `MPI_Gatherv`; timing is `MPI_Allreduce(MAX)` over ranks.\n- **CUDA**: templated CSR kernel where a group of `THREADS_PER_ROW` lanes cooperates on one row and reduces with `__shfl_down_sync` over a correct per-group mask. Group size (2/4/8/16/32) is picked at launch from the average row length, so accesses stay coalesced without idle lanes on short rows. `vec` goes through `__ldg`. One untimed warm-up launch precedes the timed loop.\n- **Hybrid GPU-per-thread**: each rank claims `deviceCount / ranks_on_node` GPUs (node detected with `MPI_Comm_split_type`) and drives one GPU per OpenMP thread on its own stream, sub-partitioning its rows by nnz. So all 4 GPUs are used whether you launch `-np 1` or `-np 4`; with more ranks than GPUs they share round-robin.\n- **OpenMP** also parallelizes the reference `spmvCpu` and `verifyResults` (the latter reduces to the *smallest* failing index so the diagnostic line matches the sequential scan).\n\n**`spmv/CMakeLists.txt`** — CXX+CUDA project, `native` CUDA arch, `find_package` for MPI/OpenMP/CUDAToolkit, and `set_source_files_properties(spmv.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)` so the existing `.cpp` compiles with nvcc without adding files. Executable name unchanged; no new dependencies.\n\n## Measured (4× RTX 3090, `-n 20000 -i 200`)\n\n| config | GFLOPS |\n|---|---|\n| 1 GPU (`CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0`) | 140 |\n| 4 GPUs, `-np 1` | 541 |\n| 4 GPUs, `-np 2` / `-np 4` | 510 |\n| 4 GPUs, `-np 8` (oversubscribed) | 420 |\n\nThat's ~95% of the aggregate memory-bandwidth roofline for CSR SpMV, so the kernel is essentially at the hardware limit. `-v` passes for all of these and for edge cases (`-n 1` with 4 ranks, `-n 7 -s 3`, `-n 100 -s 200`, defaults). Small/short runs are launch- and clock-ramp-bound rather than bandwidth-bound.\n\n**One deviation worth flagging:** the `-r` output's FNV hash no longer matches a serial build, because the per-row warp reduction reassociates the additions. The Kahan `Sum`, `Min`/`Max` and all five samples matched the serial reference to all 17 printed digits at the default size, and the benchmark's own criterion (`MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02`) passes everywhere. Bit-exact output would require one thread per row summing in index order — uncoalesced and several times slower — so I kept the fast reduction; say the word if hash equality matters more than throughput."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":307,"cache_read_input_tokens":57190,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":307},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"148dfd99-763e-4a1c-9495-722c3856b51e","uuid":"ec6b7c63-195a-43d0-ba87-34c6fbb474ff","timestamp":"2026-09-03T07:14:14.542Z","request_id":"req_011Ceg6KiqZsoVCix3Xwv8KV"}
